- [ ] **Step 3: `index.html`**

대시보드 뼈대는 HTML에 그대로 둔다. 캔버스 `#c`가 처음부터 있어야 `createVillage`가 붙는다. `data-t` 글자는 `ui.js`가 채운다.
```html
<!doctype html>
<html lang="ko">
<head>
<meta charset="utf-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1,viewport-fit=cover">
<title>한 영혼의 여정</title>
<link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com">
<link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" crossorigin>
<link rel="stylesheet" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Gowun+Batang:wght@400;700&family=IBM+Plex+Sans+KR:wght@400;600&display=swap">
<script type="importmap">{ "imports": { "three": "https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/three@0.160.0/build/three.module.js" } }</script>
<style>
/* 낮은 새벽빛 종이 위 남색 글자, 금실 강조 (기획 페이지와 같은 결). 결정·멈춤은 종이 창, 죽음은 밤 */
:root{--bg:#eceef4;--card:#fbfbfd;--ink:#1d2140;--muted:#5d6280;--line:#d7dae6;--gold:#a8770a;--gold2:#e2b65e;--night:#121329;--paper:#f6efe2;
  --trace:#2a6894;--trace-bg:#dbe9f4;--health:#3c8a5a;--wealth:#a8770a;--family:#3f6aa8;--happy:#c0577a;
  --serif:'Gowun Batang','Batang','AppleMyungjo',serif;--ui:'IBM Plex Sans KR','Malgun Gothic','Apple SD Gothic Neo',system-ui,sans-serif;color-scheme:light}
*{box-sizing:border-box}
html,body{margin:0;height:100%;background:var(--bg);color:var(--ink);font:14px/1.6 var(--ui);word-break:keep-all;-webkit-text-size-adjust:100%;text-size-adjust:100%}
body{overflow:hidden}
button{font:inherit;cursor:pointer;border-radius:8px;border:1px solid var(--line);background:#fff;color:var(--ink);padding:6px 12px;min-height:36px}
button:focus-visible,textarea:focus-visible{outline:3px solid var(--gold2);outline-offset:2px}
button[aria-pressed="true"]{background:var(--ink);color:#fff;border-color:var(--ink)}
button.primary{background:var(--ink);color:#fff;border-color:var(--ink);font-weight:600;min-height:44px}
button.quiet{background:transparent;border-color:currentColor;opacity:.85}
button.link{border:none;background:none;color:var(--muted);text-decoration:underline;justify-self:center}
[hidden]{display:none!important}
h3{margin:0 0 8px;font-size:15px}
.hint{color:var(--muted);font-size:12px;margin:6px 0 0}
.chips{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px}
.badge{display:inline-block;font-size:12px;padding:3px 9px;border-radius:999px;background:var(--trace-bg);color:var(--trace);font-weight:600}
/* 삶 대시보드: 위 막대 + 세 칸 */
#app{position:fixed;inset:0;display:grid;grid-template-columns:270px minmax(0,1fr) 290px;grid-template-rows:auto minmax(0,1fr);gap:12px;
  padding:0 max(12px,env(safe-area-inset-right,0px)) calc(12px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom,0px)) max(12px,env(safe-area-inset-left,0px))}
.bar{grid-column:1/-1;display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center;gap:6px 16px;padding-top:calc(10px + env(safe-area-inset-top,0px))}
.who{display:flex;align-items:baseline;gap:8px;min-width:0}
.who .one{font-family:var(--serif);font-weight:700}
.who b{font:700 34px/1 var(--serif);font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums}
.who .when{color:var(--muted);font-size:13px;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap}
.ctl{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px;margin-left:auto}
.speeds{display:flex;gap:4px}
.prog{flex-basis:100%;height:3px;background:var(--line);border-radius:2px;overflow:hidden}
.prog i{display:block;height:100%;background:var(--gold2);width:0}
.col{min-height:0;overflow:auto;display:grid;align-content:start;gap:12px}
.card{background:var(--card);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:12px;padding:14px 16px}
.card.id .big{font-family:var(--serif);font-size:15px;margin:0 0 8px}
.stage{padding:0;overflow:hidden}
.stage-box{aspect-ratio:16/9;width:100%;background:#cfd8e6}
#c{width:100%;height:100%;display:block}
.bar-row{display:grid;grid-template-columns:42px 1fr 28px;gap:8px;align-items:center;font-size:13px}
.bar-row i{display:block;height:7px;border-radius:4px;background:var(--line);overflow:hidden}
.bar-row b{display:block;height:100%}
.s-health{background:var(--health)}.s-wealth{background:var(--wealth)}.s-family{background:var(--family)}.s-happy{background:var(--happy)}
.num{text-align:right;font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums}
.fchip{display:grid;gap:0;text-align:left;border-radius:10px;padding:6px 10px}
.fchip small{font-size:11px;color:var(--muted)}
.fchip.on{background:var(--ink);color:#fff;border-color:var(--ink)} .fchip.on small{color:#cfd3ea}
.journal{list-style:none;margin:0;padding:0}
.journal li{display:grid;grid-template-columns:52px 1fr;gap:8px;padding:7px 0;border-top:1px solid var(--line)}
.journal li:first-child{border-top:none}
.journal .ja{color:var(--muted);font-size:12px;font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums}
.journal .k-birth .jt,.journal .k-death .jt{font-weight:600}
.journal .k-decision .jt{color:var(--ink)}
.journal .k-event .jt{color:var(--gold)}
.journal .k-stat .jt{color:var(--trace)}
.journal .k-note .jt{font-family:var(--serif);font-style:italic}
.peers{list-style:none;margin:0;padding:0;display:grid;gap:6px;font-size:13px}
.lmap{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(10,1fr);gap:3px}
.lmap i{aspect-ratio:1;border-radius:3px;background:var(--line)}
.lmap i.on{background:rgba(168,119,10,var(--o))}
.lmap i.e0{outline:2px solid var(--ink);outline-offset:-2px}
.fields{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(150px,1fr));gap:10px 16px;margin:12px 0}
.fields div{min-width:0} .fields dt{font-size:12px;color:var(--muted)} .fields dd{margin:0;font-weight:600}
.fields.sm{grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:6px 10px;margin:0}
/* 결정·멈춤 창 */
#modal{position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:20;display:grid;place-items:center;padding:16px;background:rgba(18,19,41,.45)}
#modal.dark{background:var(--night);color:var(--paper)}
.dlg{width:100%;max-width:560px;max-height:calc(100vh - 32px);overflow:auto;background:var(--card);border-radius:14px;padding:0 20px 18px;display:grid;gap:10px;box-shadow:0 16px 48px rgba(0,0,0,.3)}
.dlg-top{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;gap:8px;padding:12px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);color:var(--gold);font-size:13px;font-weight:600}
.dlg h3{font:700 22px/1.3 var(--serif);margin:4px 0 0}
.dlg .ctx{margin:0;font:16px/1.7 var(--serif)}
.dlg .note{margin:0;padding:8px 12px;border-left:3px solid var(--trace);background:#f0f4f9;font-size:12px;color:var(--muted)}
.dlg .q{font:16px/1.6 var(--serif);margin:4px 0 0}
.dlg textarea{width:100%;font:inherit;padding:8px;border-radius:8px;border:1px solid var(--line)}
.opts{list-style:none;margin:0;padding:0;display:grid;gap:8px}
.opts button{width:100%;display:grid;grid-template-columns:22px 1fr;gap:2px 10px;text-align:left;padding:10px 12px;border-radius:10px;background:#f3f4f8}
.opts .n{color:var(--muted);grid-row:span 2}
.opts .t{font-weight:600}
.opts .h{grid-column:2;font-size:12px;color:var(--muted)}
.autoline{margin:0;font-size:12px;color:var(--gold);min-height:1em}
.btns{display:grid;gap:10px;max-width:420px}
.btns.row{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;max-width:none}
.check{font-size:12px;color:var(--muted);display:flex;gap:6px;align-items:center}
.last{display:grid;gap:10px;text-align:center;max-width:560px}
.last h2{font:700 36px/1.2 var(--serif);margin:0}
.last .ghost{position:fixed;inset:0;opacity:0}
/* 서류(출생·사망 기록)와 시작·족보 화면 */
#screen{position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:10;overflow:auto;background:var(--bg);padding:calc(20px + env(safe-area-inset-top,0px)) max(16px,env(safe-area-inset-right,0px)) calc(24px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom,0px)) max(16px,env(safe-area-inset-left,0px))}
#screen.night{background:var(--night);color:var(--paper)}
.doc{max-width:760px;margin:0 auto;background:var(--card);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:12px;overflow:hidden}
.doc-head{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;gap:8px;padding:12px 20px;background:var(--ink);color:var(--gold2);font-weight:600}
.doc.death .doc-head{background:#0d0e1f}
.doc-body{padding:18px 20px 22px;display:grid;gap:10px}
.doc h2{font:700 24px/1.35 var(--serif);margin:0;text-wrap:balance}
.doc .obit{font:16px/1.8 var(--serif);margin:0}
.doc .quote{font-family:var(--serif);font-style:italic;margin:0}
.doc textarea{width:100%;font:inherit;padding:8px;border-radius:8px;border:1px solid var(--line);margin-top:6px}
.mono{font-family:ui-monospace,Consolas,monospace;font-size:12px}
.eyebrow{color:var(--gold);letter-spacing:.06em;font-size:12px;font-weight:600;margin:0}
.sheet{max-width:720px;margin:0 auto;display:grid;gap:18px}
.sheet h1{font:700 clamp(32px,6vw,52px)/1.2 var(--serif);margin:0;text-wrap:balance}
.night button:not(.primary){background:transparent;color:var(--paper);border-color:rgba(246,239,226,.5)}
.night button.primary{background:var(--gold2);border-color:var(--gold2);color:#241c08}
.night .eyebrow{color:var(--gold2)}
.credit{font-size:12px;opacity:.75;max-width:62ch}
.surv{width:100%;max-width:420px;height:auto;color:var(--ink)}
.lives{display:grid;gap:14px;position:relative;padding-left:22px}
.lives::before{content:'';position:absolute;left:6px;top:8px;bottom:8px;width:2px;background:var(--gold2)}
.life{display:grid;grid-template-columns:160px 1fr;gap:12px;align-items:center;position:relative}
.life::before{content:'';position:absolute;left:-20px;top:50%;width:10px;height:10px;border-radius:50%;background:var(--gold2);transform:translateY(-50%)}
.life img{width:160px;border-radius:8px;display:block}
.dex{overflow-x:auto} .dex table{border-collapse:collapse;font-size:12px}
.dex td,.dex th{border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.15);padding:4px 6px;text-align:center;min-width:44px}
.dex td.on{background:var(--gold2);color:#241c08;font-weight:600}
#intro{position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:30;background:var(--night);transition:opacity .8s}
#intro canvas{position:absolute;inset:0;width:100%;height:100%}
#intro .it{position:absolute;left:50%;bottom:calc(10% + env(safe-area-inset-bottom,0px));transform:translateX(-50%);width:min(560px,92vw);text-align:center;display:grid;gap:8px;background:rgba(246,239,226,.95);color:var(--ink);border-radius:14px;padding:16px 18px;transition:opacity .6s}
#intro .big{font:700 30px/1.2 var(--serif)} #intro .count{font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums;color:var(--muted);font-size:13px}
#toast{position:fixed;z-index:40;left:50%;bottom:calc(20px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom,0px));transform:translateX(-50%);background:var(--ink);color:#fff;padding:8px 14px;border-radius:999px}
@media (prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){#intro,#intro .it{transition:none}}
/* 좁은 화면: 한 줄로 쌓고 문서처럼 스크롤 */
@media (max-width:900px){
  body{overflow:auto}
  #app{position:static;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:10px}
  .bar{position:sticky;top:0;z-index:5;background:var(--bg);padding-bottom:6px}
  .ctl{margin-left:0;overflow-x:auto;flex-wrap:nowrap;max-width:100%}
  .col{overflow:visible}
  .mid{order:1}.left{order:2}.right{order:3}
  .life{grid-template-columns:110px 1fr}.life img{width:110px}
}
</style>
</head>
<body>
<div id="app" hidden>
  <header class="bar">
    <div class="who"><span class="one" data-t="oneIn"></span><b id="age">0</b><span data-t="unit"></span><span id="when" class="when"></span></div>
    <div class="ctl" role="group">
      <button id="bPlay"></button>
      <span id="speeds" class="speeds"><button data-speed="1">1×</button><button data-speed="2">2×</button><button data-speed="4">4×</button><button data-speed="8">8×</button></span>
      <button id="bSkip" data-t="skip"></button>
      <button id="bAuto" data-t="auto" aria-pressed="false"></button>
      <button id="bSound"></button>
      <button id="bLang" data-t="lang"></button>
      <button id="bExit" data-t="exit"></button>
    </div>
    <div class="prog"><i id="prog"></i></div>
  </header>
  <aside class="col left">
    <section class="card id"><h3 data-t="birthDoc"></h3><div id="idBody"></div></section>
    <section class="card"><h3 data-t="statsTitle"></h3><div id="statBars"></div><div id="traceChips" class="chips" style="margin-top:8px"></div></section>
  </aside>
  <main class="col mid">
    <section class="card stage"><div class="stage-box"><canvas id="c"></canvas></div></section>
    <section class="card"><h3 data-t="focusTitle"></h3><div id="focusBox"></div></section>
    <section class="card"><h3 data-t="journal"></h3><ol id="journal" class="journal"></ol></section>
  </main>
  <aside class="col right">
    <section class="card"><h3 data-t="peersTitle"></h3><ul id="peerList" class="peers"></ul></section>
    <section class="card"><h3 data-t="mapTitle"></h3><div id="lifeMap"></div><p class="hint" data-t="mapHint"></p></section>
  </aside>
</div>
<section id="screen" hidden></section>
<div id="modal" hidden></div>
<div id="intro" hidden><canvas id="souls"></canvas><div class="it" id="introText"></div></div>
<div id="toast" hidden></div>
<script type="module" src="./main.js"></script>
</body>
</html>
```

- [ ] **Step 4: `ui.js`**

```js
// 화면 (설계 8·12장, 레퍼런스 1in8b): 시작 → 탄생 장면 → 출생 기록 → 삶 대시보드 → 결정·멈춤 창 → 마지막 해 → 사망 기록
// 기다리는 일은 모두 Promise. robot(?auto=1)이면 단추를 저절로 누른다
import { t, fmtYear, birthLine, fill, traceText, deathLine, causeName, effectHint, choiceHint, focusName, birthsThatYear, fmtBig, obituary, peerLine } from './i18n.js';
import { REGIONS, REGION_NAME, AREAS } from './places.js';
import { ERAS, TOTAL, eraOf } from './births.js';
import { householdOf } from './households.js';
import { lifeStats, shapeFor, survivalTo, survivalCurve } from './lifetable.js';
import { FOCI, FOCUS_EFFECT, focusStage } from './focus.js';
import { PAUSE_Q } from './lines.js';

const $ = (id) => document.getElementById(id);
const esc = (s) => String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, (c) => ({ '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' })[c]);
const STAT_KEYS = ['health', 'wealth', 'family', 'happy'];
const okImg = (s) => typeof s === 'string' && s.startsWith('data:image/');

export function createUI({ lang, robot = false, fast = false }) {
  let L = lang, autoOn = false, h = {}, shown = 0, cur = null;
  const app = $('app'), screen = $('screen'), modal = $('modal'), intro = $('intro'), toast = $('toast');
  const show = (el, html) => { el.innerHTML = html; el.hidden = false; };
  const badge = (tr) => `<span class="badge">${esc(traceText(tr, L))}</span>`;
  const field = (k, v) => `<div><dt>${esc(k)}</dt><dd>${esc(v)}</dd></div>`;
  // root 안 [data-v] 단추를 누를 때까지. auto 가 있으면 그 단추를 곧 누른다 ('first' = 첫 단추)
  function pick(root, auto = robot ? 'first' : null) {
    return new Promise((res) => {
      const on = (e) => { const b = e.target.closest('[data-v]'); if (!b || !root.contains(b)) return; root.removeEventListener('click', on); res(b.dataset.v); };
      root.addEventListener('click', on);
      if (auto != null) setTimeout(() => { const b = auto === 'first' ? root.querySelector('[data-v]') : root.querySelector(`[data-v="${auto}"]`); if (b) b.click(); }, fast ? 20 : 350);
    });
  }
  function say(msg) { toast.textContent = msg; toast.hidden = false; clearTimeout(say.t); say.t = setTimeout(() => (toast.hidden = true), 2200); }
  function labels() { for (const el of document.querySelectorAll('[data-t]')) el.textContent = t(L, el.dataset.t); }

  // 위 막대·초점 단추 (index.html 에 있다. 한 번만 묶는다)
  $('bPlay').addEventListener('click', () => h.play && h.play());
  $('speeds').addEventListener('click', (e) => { const b = e.target.closest('[data-speed]'); if (b && h.speed) h.speed(+b.dataset.speed); });
  $('bSkip').addEventListener('click', () => h.skip && h.skip());
  $('bAuto').addEventListener('click', () => { autoOn = !autoOn; if (h.auto) h.auto(autoOn); });
  $('bSound').addEventListener('click', () => h.sound && h.sound());
  $('bLang').addEventListener('click', () => h.lang && h.lang());
  $('bExit').addEventListener('click', () => h.exit && h.exit());
  $('focusBox').addEventListener('click', (e) => { const b = e.target.closest('[data-focus]'); if (b && h.focus) h.focus(b.dataset.focus === 'auto' ? null : b.dataset.focus); });

  async function title({ soul, dexCount, onLang, onSound, soundOn }) {
    app.hidden = true; modal.hidden = true; screen.className = 'night';
    let sure = false;
    for (;;) {
      const has = soul && soul.lives.length > 0, going = has && !soul.ended;
      show(screen, `<div class="sheet">
        <p class="eyebrow">${esc(t(L, 'oneIn'))}</p><h1>${esc(t(L, 'title'))}</h1><p>${esc(t(L, 'subtitle'))}</p>
        <div class="btns">
          <button class="primary" data-v="start">${esc(going ? t(L, 'next', soul.lives.length + 1) : t(L, 'born'))}</button>
          ${has ? `<button data-v="lineage">${esc(t(L, 'lineage'))}</button>` : ''}
          <button data-v="dex">${esc(t(L, 'dex', dexCount))}</button>
          ${has ? `<button class="quiet" data-v="${sure ? 'newsoul' : 'ask'}">${esc(sure ? t(L, 'newSoulSure') : t(L, 'newSoul'))}</button>` : ''}
        </div>
        <div class="btns row"><button class="quiet" data-v="lang">${esc(t(L, 'lang'))}</button><button class="quiet" data-v="sound">${esc(soundOn() ? t(L, 'soundOn') : t(L, 'soundOff'))}</button></div>
        <p class="credit">${esc(t(L, 'credit'))}</p></div>`);
      const v = await pick(screen);
      if (v === 'lang') { L = L === 'ko' ? 'en' : 'ko'; onLang(L); continue; }
      if (v === 'sound') { onSound(); continue; }
      if (v === 'ask') { sure = true; continue; }
      screen.hidden = true;
      return v;
    }
  }

  // 탄생 장면: 영혼 2만 개 가운데 하나가 금실을 끌며 떨어진다
  function birthIntro({ birth, n, gap, traces }) {
    app.hidden = true; screen.hidden = true; modal.hidden = true;
    intro.hidden = false; intro.style.opacity = '1';
    const cv = $('souls'), w = (cv.width = window.innerWidth), hh = (cv.height = window.innerHeight), g = cv.getContext('2d');
    const sky = document.createElement('canvas'); sky.width = w; sky.height = hh;
    const sg = sky.getContext('2d');
    sg.fillStyle = '#121329'; sg.fillRect(0, 0, w, hh);
    for (let i = 0; i < 20000; i++) {   // 비스듬한 띠에 몰리게
      const u = Math.random(), band = (Math.random() + Math.random() + Math.random()) / 3 - 0.5, b = Math.random();
      sg.fillStyle = `rgba(255,${(200 + b * 40) | 0},150,${(0.25 + b * 0.5).toFixed(2)})`;
      sg.fillRect(u * w, hh * 0.42 + band * hh * 0.9 - (u - 0.5) * hh * 0.35, 1.2, 1.2);
    }
    const sx = w * (0.35 + Math.random() * 0.3), sy = hh * 0.1, ex = w / 2, ey = hh * 0.55;
    const it = $('introText');
    it.innerHTML = `<div class="big">${esc(t(L, 'oneIn'))}</div>
      <div class="count">${esc(t(L, 'everBorn'))} ${TOTAL.toLocaleString(L === 'ko' ? 'ko-KR' : 'en-US')}</div>
      ${n > 1 ? `<div>${esc(t(L, 'nth', n))}${gap != null ? ' · ' + esc(t(L, 'wander', gap)) : ''}</div>` : ''}
      <div>${esc(birthLine(birth, L))}</div>
      ${traces.length ? `<div class="chips" style="justify-content:center">${traces.map(badge).join('')}</div>` : ''}`;
    it.style.opacity = '0';
    const T = fast ? 1 : 2600;
    return new Promise((res) => {
      const t0 = performance.now();
      let done = false;
      const finish = () => {
        if (done) return; done = true;
        intro.removeEventListener('click', finish);
        intro.style.opacity = '0';
        setTimeout(() => { intro.hidden = true; res(); }, fast ? 0 : 800);
      };
      const step = (now) => {
        if (done) return;
        const k = Math.min(1, (now - t0) / T), e = 1 - (1 - k) ** 3, x = sx + (ex - sx) * e, y = sy + (ey - sy) * e;
        g.drawImage(sky, 0, 0);
        g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(226,182,94,.9)'; g.lineWidth = 2; g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(sx, sy); g.lineTo(x, y); g.stroke();
        const glow = g.createRadialGradient(x, y, 0, x, y, 18);
        glow.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255,240,200,1)'); glow.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255,200,120,0)');
        g.fillStyle = glow; g.fillRect(x - 18, y - 18, 36, 36);
        if (k >= 0.6) it.style.opacity = '1';
        if (k < 1) requestAnimationFrame(step); else setTimeout(finish, fast ? 0 : robot ? 400 : 2600);
      };
      requestAnimationFrame(step);
      intro.addEventListener('click', finish);
    });
  }

  // 출생 기록 (서류)
  function birthRecord({ birth, n, prev, peers, traces }) {
    app.hidden = true; modal.hidden = true; screen.className = '';
    const sh = shapeFor(birth.era, birth.region), e0 = Math.round(lifeStats(sh).e0), u5 = Math.round((1 - survivalTo(sh, 5)) * 100);
    show(screen, `<article class="doc"><header class="doc-head"><span>${esc(t(L, 'birthDoc'))}</span><span class="mono">No. ${esc(birth.seed)}</span></header>
      <div class="doc-body">
        <p class="eyebrow">${esc(n ? t(L, 'nth', n) : t(L, 'practice'))}</p>
        <h2>${esc(birthLine(birth, L))}</h2>
        ${prev ? `<p>${esc(t(L, 'wander', birth.year - prev.death.year))}</p>` : ''}
        ${prev && prev.note ? `<p class="quote">${esc(t(L, 'lastWords'))} · “${esc(prev.note)}”</p>` : ''}
        ${traces.length ? `<div class="chips">${traces.map(badge).join('')}</div>` : ''}
        <dl class="fields">
          ${field(t(L, 'f_era'), eraOf(birth.year)[L])}${field(t(L, 'f_year'), fmtYear(birth.year, L))}
          ${field(t(L, 'f_place'), AREAS[birth.area][L])}${field(t(L, 'f_home'), householdOf(birth.group, birth.household)[L])}
          ${field(t(L, 'f_child'), t(L, 'childOf', birth.order, birth.sex))}${field(t(L, 'f_e0'), t(L, 'age', e0))}
          ${field(t(L, 'f_u5'), u5 + '%')}${field(t(L, 'f_births'), fmtBig(birthsThatYear(birth.year), L))}
        </dl>
        <h3>${esc(t(L, 'peersTitle'))}</h3>
        <ul class="peers">${peers.map((p) => `<li>${esc(peerLine(p, null, L))}</li>`).join('')}</ul>
        <p class="hint">${esc(t(L, 'peersHint'))}</p>
        <div class="btns"><button class="primary" data-v="go">${esc(t(L, 'start'))}</button></div>
      </div></article>`);
    screen.scrollTop = 0;
    return pick(screen).then(() => { screen.hidden = true; });
  }

  // 삶 대시보드
  let lastState = null;
  function showApp(life, peers, state) {
    cur = { life, peers }; shown = 0;
    $('journal').innerHTML = '';
    screen.hidden = true; modal.hidden = true; app.hidden = false;
    labels(); update(life, peers, state);
  }
  function journalItem(life, j) {
    const li = document.createElement('li');
    li.className = 'k-' + j.kind;
    const text = j.kind === 'birth' ? birthLine(life.birth, L) : j.kind === 'death' ? deathLine(life.death, L) : (j.kind === 'decision' ? '▸ ' : '') + fill(j.text[L], life.birth, L);
    li.innerHTML = `<span class="ja">${esc(t(L, 'age', j.age))}</span><span class="jt">${esc(text)}</span>`;
    return li;
  }
  function mapHtml(life, e0) {
    const end = life.death ? life.death.age : life.age;
    const n = Math.ceil(Math.max(e0 + 1, end + 1, 10) / 10) * 10;
    let s = `<div class="lmap" role="img" aria-label="${esc(t(L, 'mapTitle'))}">`;
    for (let a = 0; a < n; a++) {
      const rec = life.history[a], lived = !!rec && a <= end;
      s += `<i class="${lived ? 'on' : ''} ${a === e0 ? 'e0' : ''}" style="--o:${lived ? (0.25 + (rec.happy / 100) * 0.75).toFixed(2) : 0}"></i>`;
    }
    return s + '</div>';
  }
  function update(life, peers, state) {
    if (state) lastState = state;
    const st = lastState, b = life.birth, sh = shapeFor(b.era, b.region), e0 = Math.round(lifeStats(sh).e0);
    $('age').textContent = life.age;
    $('when').textContent = `${fmtYear(life.year, L)} · ${eraOf(life.year)[L]} · ${AREAS[b.area].short[L]}`;
    $('prog').style.width = Math.min(100, (life.age / Math.max(e0 * 1.4, life.age + 1)) * 100) + '%';
    $('idBody').innerHTML = `<p class="big">${esc(birthLine(b, L))}</p><dl class="fields sm">${field(t(L, 'f_e0'), t(L, 'age', e0))}${field(t(L, 'f_u5'), Math.round((1 - survivalTo(sh, 5)) * 100) + '%')}</dl>`;
    $('statBars').innerHTML = STAT_KEYS.map((k) => `<div class="bar-row"><span>${esc(t(L, 'stats')[k])}</span><i><b class="s-${k}" style="width:${life.stats[k].toFixed(0)}%"></b></i><span class="num">${Math.round(life.stats[k])}</span></div>`).join('');
    $('traceChips').innerHTML = life.traces.map(badge).join('');
    const fs = focusStage(life.age), now = life.focus || 'auto';
    $('focusBox').innerHTML = life.age < 6 ? `<p class="hint">${esc(t(L, 'focusBaby'))}</p>`
      : `<div class="chips">${[['auto', t(L, 'focusAuto'), ''], ...FOCI.map((id) => [id, focusName(id, life.age, L), effectHint(FOCUS_EFFECT[id][fs], L)])]
        .map(([id, name, hint]) => `<button class="fchip ${now === id ? 'on' : ''}" data-focus="${id}" aria-pressed="${now === id}">${esc(name)}${hint ? `<small>${esc(hint)}</small>` : ''}</button>`).join('')}</div><p class="hint">${esc(t(L, 'focusHint'))}</p>`;
    const ol = $('journal');
    for (; shown < life.journal.length; shown++) ol.prepend(journalItem(life, life.journal[shown]));
    $('peerList').innerHTML = peers.map((p) => `<li>${esc(peerLine(p, life.age, L))}</li>`).join('');
    $('lifeMap').innerHTML = mapHtml(life, e0);
    if (st) {
      $('bPlay').textContent = st.paused ? '▶ ' + t(L, 'resume') : '❚❚ ' + t(L, 'pause');
      for (const btn of $('speeds').querySelectorAll('[data-speed]')) btn.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(+btn.dataset.speed === st.speed));
      $('bAuto').setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(autoOn));
      $('bSound').textContent = st.sound ? t(L, 'soundOn') : t(L, 'soundOff');
    }
  }

  // 중대 결정 창. 자동이면 5초 뒤 autoIndex 를 고른다 (창을 누르면 멈춘다)
  function decision(card, life, { badge: tr, autoIndex = 0, auto = false }) {
    const b = life.birth;
    show(modal, `<div class="dlg" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="dlgT">
      <div class="dlg-top"><span>${esc(t(L, 'decision'))}</span><span>${esc(t(L, 'age', life.age))} · ${esc(fmtYear(life.year, L))}</span></div>
      ${tr ? `<span class="badge">${esc(t(L, 'trace'))} · ${esc(traceText(tr, L))}</span>` : ''}
      <h3 id="dlgT">${esc(fill(card.title[L], b, L))}</h3>
      <p class="ctx">${esc(fill(card.text[L], b, L))}</p>
      ${card.note ? `<p class="note">${esc(card.note[L])}</p>` : ''}
      <ol class="opts">${card.choices.map((c, i) => `<li><button data-v="${i}"><span class="n">${i + 1}</span><span class="t">${esc(fill(c.text[L], b, L))}</span><span class="h">${esc(choiceHint(c, L))}</span></button></li>`).join('')}</ol>
      <p class="autoline" id="autoLine" aria-live="polite"></p>
      <button class="link" id="autoBtn">${esc(t(L, 'autoOn'))}</button>
    </div>`);
    let timer = null;
    const go = () => {
      $('autoBtn').hidden = true;
      $('autoLine').textContent = t(L, 'autoIn', fill(card.choices[autoIndex].text[L], b, L));
      timer = setTimeout(() => { const btn = modal.querySelector(`[data-v="${autoIndex}"]`); if (btn) btn.click(); }, fast ? 20 : robot ? 300 : 5000);
    };
    modal.firstElementChild.addEventListener('click', (e) => { if (timer && !e.target.closest('[data-v]')) { clearTimeout(timer); timer = null; $('autoLine').textContent = ''; } });
    $('autoBtn').addEventListener('click', (e) => { e.stopPropagation(); autoOn = true; if (h.auto) h.auto(true); go(); });
    if (autoOn || auto) go();
    const key = (e) => { const k = +e.key; if (k >= 1 && k <= card.choices.length) modal.querySelector(`[data-v="${k - 1}"]`).click(); };
    document.addEventListener('keydown', key);
    modal.querySelector('[data-v]').focus();
    return pick(modal, null).then((v) => { clearTimeout(timer); document.removeEventListener('keydown', key); modal.hidden = true; return Number(v); });
  }

  // 잠시 멈춤 (5·15·30·50·70살)
  function pause(life, { peers, prev }) {
    const a = life.age, p = Math.round(survivalTo(shapeFor(life.birth.era, life.birth.region), a) * 100);
    const prevLine = !prev ? '' : prev.death.age < a ? t(L, 'prevDead', prev.death.age) : t(L, 'prevAlive', fmtYear(prev.birth.year + a, L), AREAS[prev.birth.area].short[L]);
    show(modal, `<div class="dlg" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="pT">
      <div class="dlg-top"><span>${esc(t(L, 'pauseTitle'))}</span><span>${esc(t(L, 'age', a))} · ${esc(fmtYear(life.year, L))}</span></div>
      <h3 id="pT">${esc(t(L, 'age', a))}</h3>
      <p>${esc(t(L, 'pauseStat', p, a))}</p>
      ${prevLine ? `<p>${esc(prevLine)}</p>` : ''}
      <p class="hint">${esc(t(L, 'peersTitle'))} — ${peers.map((x) => esc(peerLine(x, a, L))).join(' · ')}</p>
      <p class="q">${esc(PAUSE_Q[a][L])}</p>
      <textarea id="noteBox" maxlength="140" rows="2" placeholder="${esc(t(L, 'notePh'))}"></textarea>
      <div class="btns row"><button class="primary" data-v="note">${esc(t(L, 'noteWrite'))}</button><button data-v="keep">${esc(t(L, 'noteKeep'))}</button></div>
      <label class="check"><input type="checkbox" id="pauseOff"> ${esc(t(L, 'pauseOff'))}</label>
    </div>`);
    return pick(modal, robot ? 'keep' : null).then((v) => {
      const note = v === 'note' ? $('noteBox').value.trim() : '', off = $('pauseOff').checked;
      modal.hidden = true;
      return { note, off };
    });
  }

  // 마지막 해 (검은 화면, 누르면 넘어간다)
  function lastYear(life) {
    const d = life.death, b = life.birth;
    modal.classList.add('dark');
    show(modal, `<div class="last" role="dialog" aria-modal="true"><h2>${esc(t(L, 'lastYear', d.age))}</h2>
      <p>${esc(t(L, 'diedIn', AREAS[b.area].short[L]))}</p><p class="hint">${esc(t(L, 'cause'))} · ${esc(causeName(d.cause, L))}</p>
      <p>${esc(deathLine(d, L))}</p><p class="hint">${esc(t(L, 'tapNext'))}</p></div>`);
    return new Promise((res) => {
      const fin = () => { modal.removeEventListener('click', fin); modal.classList.remove('dark'); modal.hidden = true; res(); };
      modal.addEventListener('click', fin);
      if (robot) setTimeout(fin, fast ? 20 : 600);
    });
  }

  function survSvg(sh, at) {
    const S = survivalCurve(sh, 100), W = 320, H = 110, k = Math.min(100, at);
    const pts = S.map((v, a) => `${((a / 100) * W).toFixed(1)},${(H - v * H).toFixed(1)}`).join(' ');
    return `<svg class="surv" viewBox="-6 -6 ${W + 12} ${H + 26}" role="img" aria-label="${esc(t(L, 'survHint'))}">
      <line x1="0" y1="${H}" x2="${W}" y2="${H}" stroke="currentColor" stroke-opacity=".25"/>
      <polyline fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" points="${pts}"/>
      <circle cx="${((k / 100) * W).toFixed(1)}" cy="${(H - S[k] * H).toFixed(1)}" r="5" fill="#e2b65e"/>
      <text x="0" y="${H + 16}" font-size="10" fill="currentColor">0</text><text x="${W - 18}" y="${H + 16}" font-size="10" fill="currentColor">100</text></svg>
      <p class="hint">${esc(t(L, 'survHint'))}</p>`;
  }

  // 사망 기록 (서류). 돌려주는 값: {act: 'next'|'lineage'|'share'|'title', note}
  function deathRecord({ life, sum, soul, peers }) {
    app.hidden = true; screen.className = '';
    const b = life.birth, d = life.death, sh = shapeFor(b.era, b.region), e0 = Math.round(lifeStats(sh).e0);
    const outlived = Math.round(survivalTo(sh, d.age + 1) * 100), longer = peers.filter((p) => p.death.age > d.age).length, ended = soul && soul.ended;
    const first = soul ? `<button class="primary" data-v="${ended ? 'lineage' : 'next'}">${esc(ended ? t(L, 'toLineage') : t(L, 'nextBtn'))}</button>`
      : `<button class="primary" data-v="title">${esc(t(L, 'toTitle'))}</button>`;
    show(screen, `<article class="doc death"><header class="doc-head"><span>${esc(t(L, 'deathDoc'))}</span><span class="mono">No. ${esc(b.seed)}</span></header>
      <div class="doc-body">
        <p class="eyebrow">${esc(soul ? t(L, 'nth', soul.lives.length) : t(L, 'practice'))}</p>
        <h2>${esc(fmtYear(b.year, L))} – ${esc(fmtYear(d.year, L))} · ${esc(t(L, 'age', d.age))} · ${esc(AREAS[b.area].short[L])}</h2>
        <p class="obit">${esc(obituary(life, sum, L))}</p>
        <p>${esc(t(L, 'cause'))} · ${esc(causeName(d.cause, L))}. ${esc(t(L, 'outlived', outlived))}</p>
        <dl class="fields">${field(t(L, 'happiest'), t(L, 'age', sum.happiest))}${field(t(L, 'topFocus'), sum.topFocus ? focusName(sum.topFocus, 30, L) : '—')}
          ${field(t(L, 'partner'), sum.married ? t(L, 'partnerYes') : t(L, 'partnerNo'))}${field(t(L, 'kids'), t(L, 'kidsN', sum.kids))}</dl>
        <h3>${esc(t(L, 'mapTitle'))}</h3>${mapHtml(life, e0)}
        <h3>${esc(t(L, 'survTitle'))}</h3>${survSvg(sh, d.age)}
        <h3>${esc(t(L, 'peersTitle'))}</h3><ul class="peers">${peers.map((p) => `<li>${esc(peerLine(p, 999, L))}</li>`).join('')}</ul><p>${esc(t(L, 'peersOut', longer))}</p>
        ${soul ? `<h3>${esc(t(L, 'gained'))}</h3><div class="chips">${soul.traces.length ? soul.traces.map(badge).join('') : esc(t(L, 'noneGained'))}</div>` : ''}
        ${ended ? `<p class="obit"><b>${esc(t(L, 'epilogue'))}</b></p>` : ''}
        ${soul ? `<label>${esc(t(L, 'toLife'))}<textarea id="toLife" maxlength="140" rows="2" placeholder="${esc(t(L, 'toLifePh'))}"></textarea></label>` : ''}
        <div class="btns">${first}<button data-v="share">${esc(t(L, 'share'))}</button>
          ${soul && !ended ? `<button data-v="lineage">${esc(t(L, 'lineage'))}</button>` : ''}${soul ? `<button class="quiet" data-v="title">${esc(t(L, 'toTitle'))}</button>` : ''}</div>
      </div></article>`);
    screen.scrollTop = 0;
    return pick(screen).then((v) => { const box = $('toLife'); const note = box ? box.value.trim() : ''; screen.hidden = true; return { act: v, note }; });
  }

  function lineage(soul) {
    app.hidden = true; screen.className = 'night';
    show(screen, `<div class="sheet"><p class="eyebrow">${esc(t(L, 'livesCount', soul.lives.length))}</p><h1>${esc(t(L, 'lineageTitle'))}</h1>
      <div class="lives">${soul.lives.map((x, i) => `<div class="life">${okImg(x.thumb) ? `<img src="${x.thumb}" alt="">` : '<div></div>'}<div><b>${esc(t(L, 'nth', i + 1))}</b><br>${esc(birthLine(x.birth, L))}<br>${esc(t(L, 'age', x.death.age))} · ${esc(causeName(x.death.cause, L))}${x.note ? `<br><i>“${esc(x.note)}”</i>` : ''}${x.gained.length ? `<div class="chips">${x.gained.map(badge).join('')}</div>` : ''}</div></div>`).join('')}
      ${soul.ended ? `<div class="life"><div></div><div><b>${esc(t(L, 'epilogue'))}</b></div></div>` : ''}</div>
      <div class="btns"><button class="primary" data-v="title">${esc(t(L, 'toTitle'))}</button>${soul.ended ? `<button data-v="newsoul">${esc(t(L, 'newSoul'))}</button>` : ''}</div></div>`);
    return pick(screen).then((v) => { screen.hidden = true; return v; });
  }

  function dex(cells) {
    app.hidden = true; screen.className = 'night';
    show(screen, `<div class="sheet"><h1>${esc(t(L, 'dexTitle'))}</h1><p>${esc(t(L, 'dexHint'))} · ${Object.keys(cells).length} / ${ERAS.length * REGIONS.length}</p>
      <div class="dex"><table><thead><tr><th></th>${REGIONS.map((r) => `<th>${esc(REGION_NAME[r][L])}</th>`).join('')}</tr></thead><tbody>
      ${ERAS.map((e) => `<tr><th>${esc(e[L])}</th>${REGIONS.map((r) => { const n = cells[`${e.id}|${r}`]; return `<td class="${n ? 'on' : ''}">${n || ''}</td>`; }).join('')}</tr>`).join('')}
      </tbody></table></div><div class="btns"><button class="primary" data-v="title">${esc(t(L, 'toTitle'))}</button></div></div>`);
    return pick(screen).then(() => { screen.hidden = true; });
  }

  function shareText(life, soul) {
    const b = life.birth, d = life.death;
    return [t(L, 'title') + (soul ? ' · ' + t(L, 'nth', soul.lives.length) : ''), birthLine(b, L), `${t(L, 'age', d.age)} · ${causeName(d.cause, L)}`, `${t(L, 'seed')} ${b.seed}`].join('\n');
  }
  // 1080×1350 카드 그림 (웹 링크에서는 내려받기가 막혀 그림을 띄우기만 한다)
  async function shareImage(thumb, text) {
    const c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = 1080; c.height = 1350;
    const g = c.getContext('2d');
    g.fillStyle = '#121329'; g.fillRect(0, 0, 1080, 1350);
    if (okImg(thumb)) { const im = new Image(); im.src = thumb; await im.decode().catch(() => {}); g.drawImage(im, 40, 40, 1000, 562); }
    g.fillStyle = '#e2b65e'; g.font = '700 54px "Gowun Batang", serif'; g.fillText(t(L, 'oneIn'), 60, 700);
    g.fillStyle = '#f6efe2'; g.font = '40px "Gowun Batang", serif';
    let y = 780;
    for (const para of text.split('\n')) {
      let line = '';
      for (const word of para.split(' ')) {
        const test = line ? line + ' ' + word : word;
        if (g.measureText(test).width > 960 && line) { g.fillText(line, 60, y); y += 56; line = word; } else line = test;
      }
      g.fillText(line, 60, y); y += 64;
    }
    return c.toDataURL('image/png');
  }
  async function share(life, soul, thumb) {
    if (robot) return;
    const text = shareText(life, soul), img = await shareImage(thumb, text);
    screen.className = '';
    show(screen, `<div class="sheet"><h2>${esc(t(L, 'share'))}</h2><pre style="white-space:pre-wrap;font:inherit;margin:0">${esc(text)}</pre>
      <div class="btns"><button class="primary" data-v="copy">${esc(t(L, 'copyText'))}</button><button class="quiet" data-v="close">${esc(t(L, 'close'))}</button></div>
      <p class="credit">${esc(t(L, 'imageHint'))}</p><img src="${img}" alt="" style="max-width:100%;border-radius:12px"></div>`);
    for (;;) {
      const v = await pick(screen, null);
      if (v === 'copy') { try { await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text); say(t(L, 'copied')); } catch { say(t(L, 'copyFail')); } continue; }
      screen.hidden = true;
      return;
    }
  }

  return {
    setHandlers: (x) => { h = x; },
    setLang(l) { L = l; labels(); if (cur && !app.hidden) { shown = 0; $('journal').innerHTML = ''; update(cur.life, cur.peers); } },
    title, birthIntro, birthRecord, showApp, update, decision, pause, lastYear, deathRecord, lineage, dex, share,
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: `main.js`**

```js
// 연결 (설계 12장): 시작 → 탄생 장면 → 출생 기록 → 해가 저절로 흐르는 한 생 → 마지막 해 → 사망 기록 → 다음 생 / 족보
// ?seed=문자열: 그 시드로 연습 한 판 (저장 안 함) · ?auto=1: 단추·결정을 저절로 · ?fast=1: 기다림 없음
import { createVillage, phaseOf } from './village.js';
import { loadHex } from './assets.js';
import { createUI } from './ui.js';
import { createSound } from './sound.js';
import { newSoul, finishLife, addDex, makeStore } from './soul.js';
import { drawBirth } from './birth.js';
import { createLife, advance, choose, summarize, setFocus, addNote, autoChoice } from './life.js';
import { makePeers } from './peers.js';
import { badgeFor } from './traces.js';
import { DECK } from './deck.js';
import { LINES } from './lines.js';

const Q = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
const robot = Q.has('auto'), fast = Q.has('fast');
let practice = Q.get('seed');
const store = makeStore();
const S = {
  lang: store.loadLang() || ((navigator.language || '').toLowerCase().startsWith('ko') ? 'ko' : 'en'), sound: store.loadSound() !== false,
  speed: 1, paused: false, skipping: false, auto: false, exit: false, pausesOff: store.loadPauses() === false,
};
const YEAR_MS = { 1: 8000, 2: 4000, 4: 2000, 8: 1000 };   // 1년 = 1배속 8초
document.documentElement.lang = S.lang;
const ui = createUI({ lang: S.lang, robot, fast });
const village = createVillage(document.getElementById('c'));
village.fast = fast;
const sound = createSound(() => S.sound);
loadHex().then((kit) => { if (kit) village.setKit(kit); });
const wait = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
let cur = null;   // 지금 사는 생 { life, peers }
window.__pl = { ui, village, S, store };

const refresh = () => { if (cur) ui.update(cur.life, cur.peers, S); };
ui.setHandlers({
  play: () => { S.paused = !S.paused; refresh(); },
  speed: (n) => { S.speed = n; S.paused = false; refresh(); },
  skip: () => { S.skipping = true; S.paused = false; refresh(); },
  auto: (on) => { S.auto = on; refresh(); },
  sound: () => { S.sound = !S.sound; store.saveSound(S.sound); refresh(); },
  lang: () => { S.lang = S.lang === 'ko' ? 'en' : 'ko'; store.saveLang(S.lang); document.documentElement.lang = S.lang; ui.setLang(S.lang); refresh(); },
  exit: () => { S.exit = true; S.paused = false; },
  focus: (id) => { if (cur) { setFocus(cur.life, id); refresh(); } },
});

// 한 해 기다리기: 속도·멈춤·건너뛰기를 그때그때 따른다 (건너뛰기는 100배)
function yearWait() {
  if (fast) return Promise.resolve();
  return new Promise((res) => {
    let done = 0, last = performance.now();
    const step = () => {
      const now = performance.now();
      if (!S.paused) done += (now - last) * (S.skipping ? 100 : 1);
      last = now;
      if (done >= YEAR_MS[S.speed] || S.exit) return res();
      setTimeout(step, 50);
    };
    step();
  });
}

// 한 생을 끝까지. 돌려주는 값: 'next' | 'lineage' | 'title'
async function playLife(soul) {
  const birth = soul ? soul.next : drawBirth(practice);
  const prev = soul && soul.lives.length ? soul.lives[soul.lives.length - 1] : null;
  const traces = soul ? soul.traces : [];
  const n = soul ? soul.lives.length + 1 : 0;
  const life = createLife({ birth, traces, quiet: S.pausesOff });
  const peers = makePeers(birth);
  window.__pl.life = life; window.__pl.soul = soul;
  village.setLife(birth); village.setAge(0, birth.year); village.setPhase('night');
  sound.chime();
  await ui.birthIntro({ birth, n, gap: prev ? birth.year - prev.death.year : null, traces });
  await ui.birthRecord({ birth, n, prev, peers, traces });
  Object.assign(S, { paused: false, skipping: false, exit: false });
  cur = { life, peers };
  ui.showApp(life, peers, S);
  village.setPhase(phaseOf(0));
  for (;;) {
    if (S.exit) { cur = null; return 'title'; }
    const ev = advance(life, DECK, { lines: LINES, yearly: true });
    village.setAge(life.age, life.year); village.setPhase(phaseOf(life.age));
    refresh();
    if (ev.type === 'death') break;
    if (ev.type === 'card') {
      S.skipping = false; refresh();
      const i = await ui.decision(ev.card, life, { badge: badgeFor(ev.card, life.traces), autoIndex: autoChoice(life, ev.card), auto: S.auto || robot });
      sound.tick(); choose(life, i); refresh();
      continue;
    }
    if (ev.type === 'pause') {
      S.skipping = false; refresh();
      const r = await ui.pause(life, { peers, prev });
      if (r.note) addNote(life, r.note);
      if (r.off) { life.quiet = true; S.pausesOff = true; store.savePauses(false); }
      refresh();
      continue;
    }
    village.tick();
    await yearWait();
  }
  cur = null;
  village.setPhase('night'); sound.bell();
  await ui.lastYear(life);
  await wait(fast ? 0 : 700);   // 밤하늘로 바뀐 뒤 찍는다
  const thumb = village.snapshot();
  const sum = summarize(life, DECK);
  if (soul) { finishLife(soul, life, { thumb }); store.saveSoul(soul); store.saveDex(addDex(store.loadDex(), life)); }
  window.__pl.done = (window.__pl.done || 0) + 1;
  for (;;) {
    const r = await ui.deathRecord({ life, sum, soul, peers });
    if (soul && r.note) { soul.lives[soul.lives.length - 1].note = r.note; store.saveSoul(soul); }
    if (r.act === 'share') { await ui.share(life, soul, thumb); continue; }
    return r.act;
  }
}

async function main() {
  for (;;) {
    if (practice) { await playLife(null); practice = null; history.replaceState(null, '', location.pathname); continue; }
    let soul = store.loadSoul();
    const act = await ui.title({
      soul, dexCount: Object.keys(store.loadDex()).length, soundOn: () => S.sound,
      onLang: (l) => { S.lang = l; store.saveLang(l); document.documentElement.lang = l; ui.setLang(l); },
      onSound: () => { S.sound = !S.sound; store.saveSound(S.sound); },
    });
    if (act === 'dex') { await ui.dex(store.loadDex()); continue; }
    if (act === 'lineage') { if ((await ui.lineage(soul)) === 'newsoul') store.saveSoul(newSoul()); continue; }
    if (act === 'newsoul' || !soul || soul.ended) { soul = newSoul(); store.saveSoul(soul); }
    let next = await playLife(soul);
    while (next === 'next' && !soul.ended) next = await playLife(soul);
    if (next === 'lineage' && (await ui.lineage(soul)) === 'newsoul') store.saveSoul(newSoul());
  }
}
main();
```

- [ ] **Step 6: 시험과 화면 확인**

1. Run: `cd docs/past-life && npm test` → Expected: PASS (79개)
2. 서버를 띄운다: `python tools/serve.py 8780`.
3. Playwright MCP 1280×800, 스크린샷은 `.playwright-mcp/`에 저장한다.
   - **자동 한 판:** `http://127.0.0.1:8780/index.html?seed=PLAY-1&auto=1&fast=1`
     - `window.__pl.done >= 1`이 될 때까지 기다린다(`browser_evaluate`, 최대 60초).
     - 사망 기록에 부고 글·인생 지도·생존 곡선·같은 해 사람 넷이 있다. → `pl-death.png`
   - **손으로 한 판:** `http://127.0.0.1:8780/index.html?seed=PLAY-2`
     - 탄생 장면(2초 뒤) → `pl-intro.png`
     - 누르고 출생 기록 → `pl-birth.png`
     - 「삶을 시작하기」 → 대시보드 6초 뒤 → `pl-dash.png`
       - 해가 넘어가고, 인생 기록이 위로 쌓이고, 마을 하늘이 바뀌어야 한다.
     - 「다음 결정까지」 → 결정 창 → `pl-decision.png`
       - 선택지 힌트가 보여야 한다. 1번을 누르면 인생 기록 맨 위에 「▸ 제목 · …」이 나와야 한다.
     - 다섯 살 「잠시 멈춤」 → `pl-pause.png`
       - 한 줄을 적고 「적어 두고 계속」을 누르면 인생 기록에 남아야 한다.
     - 여섯 살 이후 「몸 돌보기」를 누르면 칩이 바뀌고 몇 해 뒤 건강 막대가 올라야 한다.
     - 「English」를 누르면 위 막대·인생 기록이 영어로 바뀌어야 한다.
   - **이어지는 여정:** `http://127.0.0.1:8780/index.html?auto=1&fast=1`
     - `__pl.done >= 3`까지 기다린다.
     - `localStorage['pl.dex.v1']` 값의 합이 3 이상이어야 한다.
     - `localStorage['pl.soul.v1']`의 lives가 2개 이상이면, 뒤 생의 탄생 해가 앞 생이 죽은 해보다 뒤여야 한다.
   - 콘솔 오류 0 (`browser_console_messages`).
4. 폰 크기로 `?seed=PLAY-2`의 대시보드와 결정 창을 찍는다.
   - 세로 390×844 → `pl-phone-portrait.png`: 위 막대가 붙어 있고, 마을 → 초점 → 인생 기록 순으로 쌓인다. 가로로 넘치지 않는다.
   - 가로 844×390 → `pl-phone-landscape.png`
   - 결정 창이 화면 안에 들어가고, 단추는 36px 이상이다.
5. 스크린샷을 직접 보고 고친다: 글자 잘림, 겹침, 마을 칸 비율(16:9).

- [ ] **Step 1: `build_share.py`**

```python
# 「한 영혼의 여정」을 보내기 쉬운 HTML 파일 하나로 묶는다 (모듈 전부 + assets/hex.glb base64). three.js 와 글꼴만 인터넷
# 사용: python build_share.py  -> ../../전생시뮬_공유/플레이/pastlife-play.html (카톡용) + ../share/pastlife-play-artifact.html (웹 링크용)
import base64, json, os, re

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
ROOT = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(HERE, '..', '..'))
read = lambda *p: open(os.path.join(HERE, *p), encoding='utf-8').read()

# 의존 순서 (앞 모듈이 뒤 모듈을 쓰지 않게)
ORDER = ['rng', 'births', 'world', 'places', 'households', 'birth', 'lifetable', 'events', 'deaths', 'traces', 'cards', 'focus', 'life', 'peers', 'soul',
         'cards_life', 'cards_era', 'cards_trace', 'cards_event', 'deck', 'lines', 'i18n', 'buildings', 'assets', 'village', 'sound', 'ui']
OURS = re.compile(r"^import \{([^}]*)\} from '\./(\w+)\.js';\s*$", re.M)
THREE = "import * as THREE from 'three';"

def convert(src):
    src = src.replace(THREE + '\n', '').replace(THREE, '')
    assert not re.search(r"^import .* from '(?!\./)", src, re.M), 'unexpected import'
    return OURS.sub(lambda m: f"const {{{m.group(1)}}} = __{m.group(2)};", src)

for k, name in enumerate(ORDER + ['main']):
    for dep in OURS.findall(read(name + '.js')):
        assert dep[1] in ORDER[:k], f'{name}.js 가 쓰는 {dep[1]}.js 가 ORDER 에 없거나 뒤에 있다'

parts = []
for name in ORDER:
    body = convert(read(name + '.js'))
    names = re.findall(r'^export (?:async )?(?:function|const|let|class) (\w+)', body, re.M)
    body = re.sub(r'^export ', '', body, flags=re.M)
    assert 'export ' not in re.sub(r'//.*', '', body), name
    parts.append(f"// ----- {name}.js -----\nconst __{name} = (() => {{\n{body}\nreturn {{ {', '.join(names)} }};\n}})();\n")
bundle = THREE + '\n' + '\n'.join(parts) + '// ----- main.js -----\n' + convert(read('main.js'))

hexb = base64.b64encode(open(os.path.join(HERE, 'assets', 'hex.glb'), 'rb').read()).decode('ascii')
tag = '<script>window.__HEX = ' + json.dumps(hexb) + ';</script>\n'
html = read('index.html')
src_tag = '<script type="module" src="./main.js"></script>'
assert src_tag in html
html = html.replace(src_tag, tag + '<script type="module">\n' + bundle.replace('</script>', '<\\/script>') + '</script>')

out1 = os.path.join(ROOT, '전생시뮬_공유', '플레이', 'pastlife-play.html')
os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(out1), exist_ok=True)
open(out1, 'w', encoding='utf-8', newline='\n').write(html)
print(out1, round(os.path.getsize(out1) / 1024), 'KB')

# 웹 링크(Artifact)용: 바깥 틀과 charset·viewport 메타는 게시할 때 씌워지므로 뺀다. <title> 은 맨 앞으로
art = html
for t in ['<!doctype html>', '<html lang="ko">', '<head>', '</head>', '<body>', '</body>', '</html>', '<meta charset="utf-8">',
          '<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1,viewport-fit=cover">']:
    assert t in art, t
    art = art.replace(t, '', 1)
title = re.search(r'<title>.*?</title>\n?', art).group(0)
art = '<title>한 영혼의 여정</title>\n' + art.replace(title, '', 1).strip() + '\n'
out2 = os.path.join(ROOT, 'docs', 'share', 'pastlife-play-artifact.html')
open(out2, 'w', encoding='utf-8', newline='\n').write(art)
print(out2, round(os.path.getsize(out2) / 1024), 'KB')
```

Run: `cd docs/past-life && python build_share.py`
Expected: 두 파일이 생기고 각각 3 MB 이하다(예상 약 0.7 MB).

- [ ] **Step 2: 공유 파일 확인**

1. `python tools/serve.py 8781 "D:/Users/admin/Desktop/indigame/전생시뮬_공유/플레이"`로 띄운다.
2. Playwright로 `http://127.0.0.1:8781/pastlife-play.html?seed=PLAY-1&auto=1&fast=1`을 열고 `__pl.done >= 1`까지 기다린다.
3. 콘솔 오류가 없어야 하고 KayKit 타일이 보여야 한다(스크린샷 `pl-share.png`).

- [ ] **Step 3: 출처·안내·기록**

`docs/past-life/CREDITS.md`:
```markdown
# 출처

## 자료
- 출생 수: Population Reference Bureau, "How Many People Have Ever Lived on Earth?" 표 1 (2022-11)
- 지역·땅 인구 비율: Our World in Data `population.csv` (HYDE 3.x 등 나라별 역사 인구), `tools/make_world.py`
- 기대수명: OWID `life-expectancy` (Riley 2005, UN WPP)
- 영아·아동 사망 기준: Volk & Atkinson (2013), "Infant and child death in the human environment of evolutionary adaptation"
- 집안 비율, 사건 위험률, 사인 가중치: 게임용 어림 (사료 아님)

## 3D
- KayKit Medieval Hexagon Pack 1.0 · Kay Lousberg · CC0 1.0 · https://github.com/KayKit-Game-Assets/KayKit-Medieval-Hexagon-Pack-1.0
- 그 밖의 건물·사람은 코드로 만들었다 (`buildings.js`, `village.js`)

## AI 사용
- 코드: Claude
- 카드 글(한·영): Claude가 썼다. 사람 검토는 출품 전에 한다
```

`전생시뮬_공유/README.md`의 「기획」 표 위에 다음 표를 넣는다:
```markdown
## 플레이

| 파일 | 내용 | 웹 링크 | 마지막 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `플레이/pastlife-play.html` | 게임 첫판 (해가 흐르는 한 생 · 결정 · 잠시 멈춤 · 다음 생 · 족보 · 도감, 한/영) | (Step 4에서 채움) | 2026-10-2x |
```

`docs/ai-usage-log.md` 끝에 한 줄을 더한다:
```markdown
| 2026-10-2x | 코드 · 글 | Claude (`docs/past-life/` 전체: 시뮬·생명표·결정 카드 163장과 작은 일 줄 136개 한/영·3D 마을·대시보드, 시험, build_share.py) | 「한 영혼의 여정」 첫판 | 예 (코드·카드 글 AI 작성 공개. 3D 타일은 KayKit CC0, 자료는 PRB·OWID) |
```

- [ ] **Step 4: 웹 링크와 마무리 보고**

1. Artifact로 `docs/share/pastlife-play-artifact.html`을 게시한다(비공개, icon `game`). 받은 링크를 `전생시뮬_공유/README.md`에 적는다.
2. 사용자에게 다음을 보고한다:
   - 공유 파일 경로와 웹 링크
   - 시험 수
   - 스크린샷 5장 (출생 기록, 대시보드, 결정 창, 사망 기록, 폰)
   - 알려진 남은 일:
     - 사람 시험 (어려서 끝나는 생의 이탈)
     - 카드 글 사람 검토
     - itch 페이지
3. 커밋·저장소 만들기(예: `openbaeseongjin/past-life`)는 사용자에게 물어본다.

---

## Self-Review

**1. 설계 대조:**

| 설계 장 | 작업 |
|---|---|
| 1장 성공 기준 | 끝까지 돌아감 → 12. 비율 ±1.5%p → 1·2 시험. e0 ±0.5 → 3. 결정성 → 5. 한·영 → 9·10·12. 폰 → 12 Step 6. 공유 파일 → 13 |
| 3장 탄생 | 1, 2 |
| 4장 생명표·사인 | 3(건강 배율은 12장 값). 사건 위험률 → 5 |
| 5장 흐름·카드·흔적 | 4, 5 (한 해의 순서는 12장대로) |
| 6장 영혼 | 6 |
| 7장 내용 | 7~9(결정 카드), 10(작은 일 줄) |
| 8장 화면 | 11(마을), 12(대시보드) |
| 10장 위험 | 통계 줄(첫돌·잠시 멈춤·사망 기록), 「다음 생」이 맨 앞 단추 → 12 |
| 11장 AI 공개 | 13 |
| 12장 진행 방식 | 시간·속도·건너뛰기·자동 결정 → 12(main.js `yearWait`, ui `decision`). 한 해의 순서·초점·되돌림·작은 일·첫돌 통계 → 5. 사건 시작 줄 → 3·5. 잠시 멈춤 → 5·10·12. 같은 해 태어난 사람들 → 6·12. 출생·사망 기록·부고·인생 지도·생존 곡선 → 12. 사인 이름 → 3 |

**2. 빈칸 검사:**
- 결정 카드 163장 가운데 본보기 7장, 작은 일 줄 136개 가운데 본보기 11줄은 전문을 적었다.
- 나머지는 한 줄 요약(카드)이나 주제 목록(줄)과 규칙으로 정했다. 내용 시험이 범위·길이·key를 지킨다.
- 「2026-10-2x」는 작업한 날짜로 채운다.

**3. 이름 맞추기:**
- 앞뒤 작업에서 이름이 같다:
  - `drawBirth(seed, after)`, `birthAt(seed, year)`
  - `createLife({birth, traces, noDeath, quiet})`, `advance(life, deck, {lines, yearly})`, `choose(life, i)`, `summarize(life, deck)`, `setFocus`, `addNote`, `autoChoice`, `PAUSES`
  - `makePeers(birth)`, `peerAt(peer, age)`, `finishLife(soul, life, extra)`, `badgeFor(card, traces)`, `phaseOf(age)`
  - `village.setLife/setAge/setPhase/tick/snapshot/setKit`
  - `ui.setHandlers/setLang/title/birthIntro/birthRecord/showApp/update/decision/pause/lastYear/deathRecord/lineage/dex/share`
  - `survivalTo`, `survivalCurve`, `FOCUS_NAME`, `FOCUS_EFFECT`, `focusStage`, `LINES`, `PAUSE_Q`
- 사인 id는 `hunger`로 통일했다(설계 표의 「famine」).
- 시험 수: 10 → 17 → 31 → 39 → 56 → 65 → (7·8은 임시 검사) → 72 → 75 → 79.
